### src/contextpack/cli.py

<!-- bytes: 1439 tokens: 326 -->

```python
from pathlib import Path

import typer

from contextpack.models import PackConfig
from contextpack.pipeline import pack, write_result


def main(
    path: Path = typer.Argument(
        ...,
        exists=True,
        file_okay=False,
        dir_okay=True,
        readable=True,
        help="Directory to pack.",
    ),
    output: Path | None = typer.Option(None, "--output", "-o", help="Write digest to this file."),
    max_file_bytes: int = typer.Option(512_000, "--max-file-bytes"),
    exclude: list[str] = typer.Option([], "--exclude", "-e", help="Extra gitignore-style pattern."),
    no_gitignore: bool = typer.Option(False, "--no-gitignore"),
) -> None:
    """Pack a local directory into one markdown digest."""
    result = pack(
        PackConfig(
            root=path,
            output=output,
            max_file_bytes=max_file_bytes,
            respect_gitignore=not no_gitignore,
            extra_excludes=exclude,
        )
    )
    written = write_result(result, output)
    typer.echo(
        f"files={len(result.files)} skipped={len(result.skipped)} "
        f"bytes={result.total_bytes} tokens={result.total_tokens}"
    )
    if written is not None:
        typer.echo(f"wrote {written}")
    else:
        typer.echo(result.markdown)


def app() -> None:
    """Console script entrypoint."""
    typer.run(main)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app()
```

```python
from pathlib import Path

import pathspec

# Directories and files that are never useful as model context.
DEFAULT_EXCLUDES = [
    ".git/",
    ".hg/",
    ".svn/",
    ".idea/",
    ".vscode/",
    "node_modules/",
    "vendor/",
    "dist/",
    "build/",
    "coverage/",
    ".next/",
    ".nuxt/",
    ".venv/",
    "venv/",
    "__pycache__/",
    ".pytest_cache/",
    ".mypy_cache/",
    ".ruff_cache/",
    "*.pyc",
    "*.pyo",
    "package-lock.json",
    "pnpm-lock.yaml",
    "yarn.lock",
    "bun.lock",
    "bun.lockb",
    "Cargo.lock",
    "poetry.lock",
    "Pipfile.lock",
    "composer.lock",
    "Gemfile.lock",
]

BINARY_SUFFIXES = {
    ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".webp", ".ico", ".bmp", ".svgz",
    ".pdf", ".zip", ".tar", ".gz", ".tgz", ".bz2", ".7z", ".rar",
    ".woff", ".woff2", ".ttf", ".eot", ".otf",
    ".mp3", ".mp4", ".mov", ".avi", ".webm", ".wav",
    ".exe", ".dll", ".so", ".dylib", ".bin", ".wasm",
    ".sqlite", ".db", ".parquet", ".class", ".jar",
}

LANGUAGE_BY_SUFFIX = {
    ".py": "python",
    ".pyi": "python",
    ".js": "javascript",
    ".mjs": "javascript",
    ".cjs": "javascript",
    ".jsx": "jsx",
    ".ts": "typescript",
    ".tsx": "tsx",
    ".go": "go",
    ".rs": "rust",
    ".java": "java",
    ".kt": "kotlin",
    ".rb": "ruby",
    ".php": "php",
    ".cs": "csharp",
    ".c": "c",
    ".h": "c",
    ".cpp": "cpp",
    ".hpp": "cpp",
    ".swift": "swift",
    ".sql": "sql",
    ".sh": "bash",
    ".bash": "bash",
    ".zsh": "bash",
    ".json": "json",
    ".yaml": "yaml",
    ".yml": "yaml",
    ".toml": "toml",
    ".md": "markdown",
    ".html": "html",
    ".css": "css",
    ".scss": "scss",
}


def language_for(path: Path) -> str:
    return LANGUAGE_BY_SUFFIX.get(path.suffix.lower(), "text")


def load_spec(root: Path, respect_gitignore: bool, extra: list[str]) -> pathspec.PathSpec:
    lines = list(DEFAULT_EXCLUDES)
    lines.extend(extra)
    if respect_gitignore:
        gitignore = root / ".gitignore"
        if gitignore.is_file():
            lines.extend(gitignore.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines())
    return pathspec.PathSpec.from_lines("gitignore", lines)


def is_binary(path: Path) -> bool:
    if path.suffix.lower() in BINARY_SUFFIXES:
        return True
    try:
        sample = path.read_bytes()[:8192]
    except OSError:
        return True
    return b"\x00" in sample


def iter_source_files(root: Path, spec: pathspec.PathSpec, max_file_bytes: int):
    """Yield (absolute_path, relative_posix) for text files under root.

    Skipped relative paths are yielded as the third value when the file is rejected.
    """
    root = root.resolve()
    for path in root.rglob("*"):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        relative = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        if spec.match_file(relative):
            yield None, relative, "ignored"
            continue
        try:
            size = path.stat().st_size
        except OSError:
            yield None, relative, "unreadable"
            continue
        if size > max_file_bytes:
            yield None, relative, f"oversize:{size}"
            continue
        if is_binary(path):
            yield None, relative, "binary"
            continue
        yield path, relative, None
```

### src/contextpack/formatters.py

<!-- bytes: 1997 tokens: 445 -->

```python
from contextpack.models import FileRecord


def directory_tree(paths: list[str]) -> str:
    """Render a POSIX path list as a text tree. Paths must use forward slashes."""
    root: dict = {}
    for path in sorted(paths):
        node = root
        for part in path.split("/"):
            node = node.setdefault(part, {})

    lines: list[str] = ["."]

    def walk(node: dict, prefix: str) -> None:
        items = list(node.items())
        for index, (name, child) in enumerate(items):
            last = index == len(items) - 1
            branch = "└── " if last else "├── "
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{branch}{name}")
            if child:
                walk(child, prefix + ("    " if last else "│   "))

    walk(root, "")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_markdown(
    root_name: str,
    files: list[FileRecord],
    skipped: list[str],
    tree: str,
    total_bytes: int,
    total_tokens: int,
) -> str:
    parts = [
        f"# Context digest: {root_name}",
        "",
        "## Summary",
        "",
        f"- Files included: {len(files)}",
        f"- Files skipped: {len(skipped)}",
        f"- Bytes: {total_bytes}",
        f"- Tokens (cl100k_base): {total_tokens}",
        "",
        "## Directory structure",
        "",
        "```text",
        tree,
        "```",
        "",
        "## Files",
        "",
    ]
    for record in files:
        parts.extend(
            [
                f"### {record.relative_path}",
                "",
                f"<!-- bytes: {record.size_bytes} tokens: {record.tokens} -->",
                "",
                f"```{record.language}",
                record.content.rstrip("\n"),
                "```",
                "",
            ]
        )
    if skipped:
        parts.extend(["## Skipped", ""])
        parts.extend(f"- {item}" for item in skipped)
        parts.append("")
    return "\n".join(parts)
```

### src/contextpack/models.py

```python
from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class PackConfig(BaseModel):
    root: Path
    output: Path | None = None
    max_file_bytes: int = 512_000
    respect_gitignore: bool = True
    extra_excludes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class FileRecord(BaseModel):
    relative_path: str
    size_bytes: int
    language: str
    content: str
    tokens: int


class PackResult(BaseModel):
    root: str
    files: list[FileRecord]
    skipped: list[str]
    tree: str
    markdown: str
    total_bytes: int
    total_tokens: int
```

### src/contextpack/pipeline.py

<!-- bytes: 2148 tokens: 444 -->

```python
from pathlib import Path

from contextpack.discover import iter_source_files, language_for, load_spec
from contextpack.formatters import directory_tree, render_markdown
from contextpack.models import FileRecord, PackConfig, PackResult
from contextpack.tokens import count_tokens


def pack(config: PackConfig) -> PackResult:
    root = config.root.expanduser().resolve()
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise NotADirectoryError(f"Not a directory: {root}")

    spec = load_spec(root, config.respect_gitignore, config.extra_excludes)
    files: list[FileRecord] = []
    skipped: list[str] = []

    for path, relative, reason in iter_source_files(root, spec, config.max_file_bytes):
        if path is None:
            skipped.append(f"{relative} ({reason})")
            continue
        content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        files.append(
            FileRecord(
                relative_path=relative,
                size_bytes=path.stat().st_size,
                language=language_for(path),
                content=content,
                tokens=count_tokens(content),
            )
        )

    files.sort(key=lambda item: item.relative_path)
    tree = directory_tree([item.relative_path for item in files])
    total_bytes = sum(item.size_bytes for item in files)
    total_tokens = sum(item.tokens for item in files)
    markdown = render_markdown(
        root_name=root.name,
        files=files,
        skipped=skipped,
        tree=tree,
        total_bytes=total_bytes,
        total_tokens=total_tokens,
    )
    return PackResult(
        root=str(root),
        files=files,
        skipped=skipped,
        tree=tree,
        markdown=markdown,
        total_bytes=total_bytes,
        total_tokens=total_tokens,
    )


def write_result(result: PackResult, output: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    if output is None:
        return None
    destination = output.expanduser().resolve()
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    destination.write_text(result.markdown, encoding="utf-8")
    return destination
```

### src/contextpack/tokens.py

```python
import tiktoken

_ENCODER = tiktoken.get_encoding("cl100k_base")


def count_tokens(text: str) -> int:
    if not text:
        return 0
    return len(_ENCODER.encode(text, disallowed_special=()))
```

### tests/test_pipeline.py

```python
from pathlib import Path

from contextpack.models import PackConfig
from contextpack.pipeline import pack


def test_pack_skips_git_noise_and_counts_real_files(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / ".git").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / ".git" / "config").write_text("bare = false\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / ".gitignore").write_text("secret.txt\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "secret.txt").write_text("do-not-include\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "node_modules").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "node_modules" / "leftpad.js").write_text("module.exports = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "package-lock.json").write_text("{}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "logo.png").write_bytes(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n\x00\x00")
    source = tmp_path / "src"
    source.mkdir()
    (source / "app.py").write_text("def hello() -> str:\n    return 'hi'\n", encoding="utf-8")

    result = pack(PackConfig(root=tmp_path))

    included = [item.relative_path for item in result.files]
    assert included == [".gitignore", "src/app.py"]

    app = next(item for item in result.files if item.relative_path == "src/app.py")
    assert app.language == "python"
    assert app.tokens > 0
    assert "def hello" in result.markdown
    assert "do-not-include" not in result.markdown

    # Noise must not be *included* as file content
    assert not any(item.relative_path.endswith("leftpad.js") for item in result.files)
    assert not any(item.relative_path.endswith("package-lock.json") for item in result.files)
    assert not any(item.relative_path.endswith("logo.png") for item in result.files)

    # It may appear only in the skipped list
    assert any("leftpad.js" in line and "ignored" in line for line in result.skipped)
    assert any("logo.png" in line and "binary" in line for line in result.skipped)
    assert any(line.startswith("secret.txt") for line in result.skipped)

def test_extra_exclude(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "keep.py").write_text("x = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "drop.py").write_text("y = 2\n", encoding="utf-8")
    result = pack(PackConfig(root=tmp_path, extra_excludes=["drop.py"]))
    assert [item.relative_path for item in result.files] == ["keep.py"]
```

### src/contextpack/cli.py

<!-- bytes: 1439 tokens: 326 -->

```python
from pathlib import Path

import typer

from contextpack.models import PackConfig
from contextpack.pipeline import pack, write_result


def main(
    path: Path = typer.Argument(
        ...,
        exists=True,
        file_okay=False,
        dir_okay=True,
        readable=True,
        help="Directory to pack.",
    ),
    output: Path | None = typer.Option(None, "--output", "-o", help="Write digest to this file."),
    max_file_bytes: int = typer.Option(512_000, "--max-file-bytes"),
    exclude: list[str] = typer.Option([], "--exclude", "-e", help="Extra gitignore-style pattern."),
    no_gitignore: bool = typer.Option(False, "--no-gitignore"),
) -> None:
    """Pack a local directory into one markdown digest."""
    result = pack(
        PackConfig(
            root=path,
            output=output,
            max_file_bytes=max_file_bytes,
            respect_gitignore=not no_gitignore,
            extra_excludes=exclude,
        )
    )
    written = write_result(result, output)
    typer.echo(
        f"files={len(result.files)} skipped={len(result.skipped)} "
        f"bytes={result.total_bytes} tokens={result.total_tokens}"
    )
    if written is not None:
        typer.echo(f"wrote {written}")
    else:
        typer.echo(result.markdown)


def app() -> None:
    """Console script entrypoint."""
    typer.run(main)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app()
```

```python
from pathlib import Path

import pathspec

# Directories and files that are never useful as model context.
DEFAULT_EXCLUDES = [
    ".git/",
    ".hg/",
    ".svn/",
    ".idea/",
    ".vscode/",
    "node_modules/",
    "vendor/",
    "dist/",
    "build/",
    "coverage/",
    ".next/",
    ".nuxt/",
    ".venv/",
    "venv/",
    "__pycache__/",
    ".pytest_cache/",
    ".mypy_cache/",
    ".ruff_cache/",
    "*.pyc",
    "*.pyo",
    "package-lock.json",
    "pnpm-lock.yaml",
    "yarn.lock",
    "bun.lock",
    "bun.lockb",
    "Cargo.lock",
    "poetry.lock",
    "Pipfile.lock",
    "composer.lock",
    "Gemfile.lock",
]

BINARY_SUFFIXES = {
    ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".webp", ".ico", ".bmp", ".svgz",
    ".pdf", ".zip", ".tar", ".gz", ".tgz", ".bz2", ".7z", ".rar",
    ".woff", ".woff2", ".ttf", ".eot", ".otf",
    ".mp3", ".mp4", ".mov", ".avi", ".webm", ".wav",
    ".exe", ".dll", ".so", ".dylib", ".bin", ".wasm",
    ".sqlite", ".db", ".parquet", ".class", ".jar",
}

LANGUAGE_BY_SUFFIX = {
    ".py": "python",
    ".pyi": "python",
    ".js": "javascript",
    ".mjs": "javascript",
    ".cjs": "javascript",
    ".jsx": "jsx",
    ".ts": "typescript",
    ".tsx": "tsx",
    ".go": "go",
    ".rs": "rust",
    ".java": "java",
    ".kt": "kotlin",
    ".rb": "ruby",
    ".php": "php",
    ".cs": "csharp",
    ".c": "c",
    ".h": "c",
    ".cpp": "cpp",
    ".hpp": "cpp",
    ".swift": "swift",
    ".sql": "sql",
    ".sh": "bash",
    ".bash": "bash",
    ".zsh": "bash",
    ".json": "json",
    ".yaml": "yaml",
    ".yml": "yaml",
    ".toml": "toml",
    ".md": "markdown",
    ".html": "html",
    ".css": "css",
    ".scss": "scss",
}


def language_for(path: Path) -> str:
    return LANGUAGE_BY_SUFFIX.get(path.suffix.lower(), "text")


def load_spec(root: Path, respect_gitignore: bool, extra: list[str]) -> pathspec.PathSpec:
    lines = list(DEFAULT_EXCLUDES)
    lines.extend(extra)
    if respect_gitignore:
        gitignore = root / ".gitignore"
        if gitignore.is_file():
            lines.extend(gitignore.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines())
    return pathspec.PathSpec.from_lines("gitignore", lines)


def is_binary(path: Path) -> bool:
    if path.suffix.lower() in BINARY_SUFFIXES:
        return True
    try:
        sample = path.read_bytes()[:8192]
    except OSError:
        return True
    return b"\x00" in sample


def iter_source_files(root: Path, spec: pathspec.PathSpec, max_file_bytes: int):
    """Yield (absolute_path, relative_posix) for text files under root.

    Skipped relative paths are yielded as the third value when the file is rejected.
    """
    root = root.resolve()
    for path in root.rglob("*"):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        relative = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        if spec.match_file(relative):
            yield None, relative, "ignored"
            continue
        try:
            size = path.stat().st_size
        except OSError:
            yield None, relative, "unreadable"
            continue
        if size > max_file_bytes:
            yield None, relative, f"oversize:{size}"
            continue
        if is_binary(path):
            yield None, relative, "binary"
            continue
        yield path, relative, None
```

### src/contextpack/formatters.py

<!-- bytes: 1997 tokens: 445 -->

```python
from contextpack.models import FileRecord


def directory_tree(paths: list[str]) -> str:
    """Render a POSIX path list as a text tree. Paths must use forward slashes."""
    root: dict = {}
    for path in sorted(paths):
        node = root
        for part in path.split("/"):
            node = node.setdefault(part, {})

    lines: list[str] = ["."]

    def walk(node: dict, prefix: str) -> None:
        items = list(node.items())
        for index, (name, child) in enumerate(items):
            last = index == len(items) - 1
            branch = "└── " if last else "├── "
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{branch}{name}")
            if child:
                walk(child, prefix + ("    " if last else "│   "))

    walk(root, "")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_markdown(
    root_name: str,
    files: list[FileRecord],
    skipped: list[str],
    tree: str,
    total_bytes: int,
    total_tokens: int,
) -> str:
    parts = [
        f"# Context digest: {root_name}",
        "",
        "## Summary",
        "",
        f"- Files included: {len(files)}",
        f"- Files skipped: {len(skipped)}",
        f"- Bytes: {total_bytes}",
        f"- Tokens (cl100k_base): {total_tokens}",
        "",
        "## Directory structure",
        "",
        "```text",
        tree,
        "```",
        "",
        "## Files",
        "",
    ]
    for record in files:
        parts.extend(
            [
                f"### {record.relative_path}",
                "",
                f"<!-- bytes: {record.size_bytes} tokens: {record.tokens} -->",
                "",
                f"```{record.language}",
                record.content.rstrip("\n"),
                "```",
                "",
            ]
        )
    if skipped:
        parts.extend(["## Skipped", ""])
        parts.extend(f"- {item}" for item in skipped)
        parts.append("")
    return "\n".join(parts)
```

### src/contextpack/models.py

<!-- bytes: 593 tokens: 148 -->

```python
from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class PackConfig(BaseModel):
    root: Path
    output: Path | None = None
    max_file_bytes: int = 512_000
    respect_gitignore: bool = True
    extra_excludes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class FileRecord(BaseModel):
    relative_path: str
    size_bytes: int
    language: str
    content: str
    tokens: int


class PackResult(BaseModel):
    root: str
    files: list[FileRecord]
    skipped: list[str]
    tree: str
    markdown: str
    total_bytes: int
    total_tokens: int
```

### src/contextpack/pipeline.py

<!-- bytes: 2148 tokens: 444 -->

```python
from pathlib import Path

from contextpack.discover import iter_source_files, language_for, load_spec
from contextpack.formatters import directory_tree, render_markdown
from contextpack.models import FileRecord, PackConfig, PackResult
from contextpack.tokens import count_tokens


def pack(config: PackConfig) -> PackResult:
    root = config.root.expanduser().resolve()
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise NotADirectoryError(f"Not a directory: {root}")

    spec = load_spec(root, config.respect_gitignore, config.extra_excludes)
    files: list[FileRecord] = []
    skipped: list[str] = []

    for path, relative, reason in iter_source_files(root, spec, config.max_file_bytes):
        if path is None:
            skipped.append(f"{relative} ({reason})")
            continue
        content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        files.append(
            FileRecord(
                relative_path=relative,
                size_bytes=path.stat().st_size,
                language=language_for(path),
                content=content,
                tokens=count_tokens(content),
            )
        )

    files.sort(key=lambda item: item.relative_path)
    tree = directory_tree([item.relative_path for item in files])
    total_bytes = sum(item.size_bytes for item in files)
    total_tokens = sum(item.tokens for item in files)
    markdown = render_markdown(
        root_name=root.name,
        files=files,
        skipped=skipped,
        tree=tree,
        total_bytes=total_bytes,
        total_tokens=total_tokens,
    )
    return PackResult(
        root=str(root),
        files=files,
        skipped=skipped,
        tree=tree,
        markdown=markdown,
        total_bytes=total_bytes,
        total_tokens=total_tokens,
    )


def write_result(result: PackResult, output: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    if output is None:
        return None
    destination = output.expanduser().resolve()
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    destination.write_text(result.markdown, encoding="utf-8")
    return destination
```

```python
import tiktoken

_ENCODER = tiktoken.get_encoding("cl100k_base")


def count_tokens(text: str) -> int:
    if not text:
        return 0
    return len(_ENCODER.encode(text, disallowed_special=()))
```

### tests/test_pipeline.py

<!-- bytes: 2258 tokens: 591 -->

```python
from pathlib import Path

from contextpack.models import PackConfig
from contextpack.pipeline import pack


def test_pack_skips_git_noise_and_counts_real_files(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / ".git").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / ".git" / "config").write_text("bare = false\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / ".gitignore").write_text("secret.txt\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "secret.txt").write_text("do-not-include\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "node_modules").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "node_modules" / "leftpad.js").write_text("module.exports = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "package-lock.json").write_text("{}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "logo.png").write_bytes(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n\x00\x00")
    source = tmp_path / "src"
    source.mkdir()
    (source / "app.py").write_text("def hello() -> str:\n    return 'hi'\n", encoding="utf-8")

    result = pack(PackConfig(root=tmp_path))

    included = [item.relative_path for item in result.files]
    assert included == [".gitignore", "src/app.py"]

    app = next(item for item in result.files if item.relative_path == "src/app.py")
    assert app.language == "python"
    assert app.tokens > 0
    assert "def hello" in result.markdown
    assert "do-not-include" not in result.markdown

    # Noise must not be *included* as file content
    assert not any(item.relative_path.endswith("leftpad.js") for item in result.files)
    assert not any(item.relative_path.endswith("package-lock.json") for item in result.files)
    assert not any(item.relative_path.endswith("logo.png") for item in result.files)

    # It may appear only in the skipped list
    assert any("leftpad.js" in line and "ignored" in line for line in result.skipped)
    assert any("logo.png" in line and "binary" in line for line in result.skipped)
    assert any(line.startswith("secret.txt") for line in result.skipped)

def test_extra_exclude(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "keep.py").write_text("x = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "drop.py").write_text("y = 2\n", encoding="utf-8")
    result = pack(PackConfig(root=tmp_path, extra_excludes=["drop.py"]))
    assert [item.relative_path for item in result.files] == ["keep.py"]
```
